# Skills · S · part 45

850 entries, scp to scraping-archive-fallback. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **scp**: This skill enables the LLM to securely copy files between local and remote hosts over SSH, providing functionality equivalent to the Linux `scp` tool. The skill produces a **strictly-formatted JSON output** that describes the file copy operation. The JSON output is then parsed by the `scp` skill to connect to the remote host via SSH and perform the transfer. ([yanggujun/open-axle](https://github.com/yanggujun/open-axle/tree/HEAD/src/skills/scp))
- **scp 替代**: scp 替代：结构化助手技能，覆盖需求确认→方案设计→执行产出→校验交付全流程，按领域最佳实践输出可执行结果。适用于「scp 替代」相关任务场景。 ([yemugua/agent-skills](https://github.com/yemugua/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/ai-agent/scp-alternatives))
- **scp-116k-eval**: Evaluates the scientific reasoning and problem-solving capabilities of LLMs on graduate-level higher education science problems. It measures how well models can parse and solve complex scientific questions involving formulas and equations. Use when the user wants to benchmark on SCP-116K, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/scp-116k-eval) · ★3)

- **scp-guardrail-design**: Use this skill when designing Service Control Policy guardrails for an AWS Organization, including deny-based baselines, region restriction, protection of logging and security services, and root user restriction. It triggers on requests to establish preventive controls across accounts, design an OU structure for policy inheritance, or safely test SCP changes before broad rollout. ([Misterb91123/aws-sample-ai-agents](https://github.com/Misterb91123/aws-sample-ai-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/compliance-governance/scp-guardrail-design))
- **scp-guardrails**: Write, review and lint AWS Organizations service control policies: build SCPs from a short spec (region allowlist, break-glass roles, protected logging, no root user, IMDSv2, no public S3 ACLs) under the SCP character limit, and lint any SCP for Allow statements, region denies that break global services, NotAction misuse and size. Use when asked "why did our region SCP break IAM?", to write a service control policy, or to lint a guardrail SCP before you attach it to an OU. Not for IAM identity policies (iam-least-privilege-review) or organization design (landing-zone-blast-radius). ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data/tree/HEAD/bash/scp-guardrails) · ★30 · 3 copies)
